= Path(__file__).parent / \'examples\'\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef snapshot():\\r\\n    return {str(p.resolve()): digest(p) for p in WORKSPACE.rglob(\'*\')\\r\\n            if p.is_file() and p.suffix.lower() in CODE}\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef state_path(payload):\\r\\n    key = str(payload.get(\'session_id\', \'\')) + \':\' + str(payload.get(\'agent_id\', \'\'))\\r\\n    return RUNTIME / (\'state-\' + hashlib.sha256(key.encode()).hexdigest() + \'.json\')\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef prepare(payload):\\r\\n    RUNTIME.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\r\\n    path = state_path(payload)\\r\\n False}), encoding=\'utf-8\')\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef close_state(payload):\\r\\n    path = state_path(payload)\\r\\n    if path.exists():\\r\\n        state = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding=\'utf-8\'))\\r\\n        state[\'closed\'] = True\\r\\n        path.write_text(json.dumps(state), encoding=\'utf-8\')\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef digest(path):\\r\\n text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/skill-dependency-tests/repair_dependencies.py\n+\"\"\"Targeted compare-and-swap dependency repair; keeps original backups.\"\"\"\n+from pathlib import Path\n+import hashlib\n+import json\n+import os\n+import subprocess\n+import tempfile\n+\n+ROOT = Path('C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os')\n+HOME = Path('C:/Users/Joshu')\n+REPORT = ROOT / 'audits/skills-2026-10-11-dependencies'\n+\n+RESEARCH = '''---\n+name: research-deep\n+description: Research an explicitly selected outline.yaml into structured JSON using its fields.yaml schema, bounded batches and field-coverage validation. Use for structured research batches, not ordinary knowledge questions or transcript intake.\n+---\n+\n+# Structured research with validated field coverage\n+\n+## Select the topic and authorization\n+\n+Use the user-selected topic directory containing `outline.yaml` and `fields.yaml`. If one unambiguous topic is explicitly implied, use it; if several candidates exist, ask which. Do not choose an arbitrary newest outline. Reuse the already authorized batch scope; ask before expanding it, not before each already approved batch. Research is read-only unless saving the requested local outputs. Do not make paid calls or publish results without corresponding authorization.\n+\n+## Resolve the validator before dispatch\n+\n+This package includes `scripts/validate_json.py`, recovered from Git commit `9b1a3aa3` (`skills/codex/research/validate_json.py`) and adapted to reject empty/missing inputs. Resolve `{validator_path}` from the actual installed skill directory, not a guessed home path. Its YAML contract is documented in `references/field-contract.md`.\n+\n+Use an interpreter with PyYAML. On this Windows machine the tested isolated interpreter is `C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/skill-dependency-tests/.venv/Scripts/python.exe`. Verify it before use. Portable fallback: `uv run --no-project --with pyyaml==6.0.3 python`. If neither exists, install PyYAML in a project environment outside the vault, not system Python. A package-manager operation does not prove the validator ran.\n+\n+Validate one output before starting a large batch. Require `--require-complete`: all schema fields, no unknown fields, and an `uncertain` array exactly matching fields whose values contain `[uncertain]`. This checks structure and coverage, not truth, source quality, English prose or whether citations support claims.\n+\n+## Workflow\n+\n+1. Read the selected outline's topic, items and execution configuration (`output_dir`, `batch_size`, `items_per_agent`). Resolve paths against that selected topic directory.\n+2. Resume only after validating existing result files with the current fields schema and strict mode. File existence alone is not completion. Keep invalid prior evidence and repair its output explicitly.\n+3. Dispatch within the authorized scope using researcher roles actually available in the host. Parallel work requires available slots. Each worker receives the full item, schema, unique output path and validator command. If no researcher role is available, perform bounded research directly rather than inventing a tool.\n+4. Preserve each field's source evidence in the schema's source/citation fields or a companion evidence Markdown file; never manufacture a value just to fill coverage. Mark unresolved values `[uncertain]` and list those field names in `uncertain`.\n+5. Run the validator on every result and repair failures. Keep stdout/stderr and the actual exit status. Report passing, failed and uncertain items. Do not suppress failures or infer completeness from an empty directory.\n+\n+## Worker prompt\n+\n+Substitute only the selected task's values below. `{validator_command}` is the verified interpreter/uv command, quoted script path and `--require-complete -f \"{fields_path}\" -j \"{output_path}\"`.\n+\n+```text\n+Research {item_related_info}, output structured JSON to {output_path}.\n+Read {fields_path} for every field definition.\n+Use primary sources and preserve citation evidence. All field values must be in English.\n+Mark unresolved field values [uncertain]. Add uncertain as an array of precisely those field names, or [] when none.\n+Keep every defined field. Do not invent values to satisfy coverage.\n+After saving, run:\n+{validator_command}\n+Complete only when the actual validator exits 0. Report failures and unresolved source limits.\n+```\n+\n+Use plain-language `research-deep` requests or the runtime's supported explicit skill picker. Codex explicit syntax is `$research-deep`; slash-command spellings are not portable.\n+'''\n+\n+BOOTCAMP = '''---\n+name: bootcamp-parity-audit\n+description: Compare a named LSAT U bootcamp with current Intro to LR source, product and visual benchmarks. Use for explicit bootcamp parity or shipped-quality audits, not general second-brain audits. Diagnose without implementation unless requested.\n+---\n+\n+# Compare a bootcamp against current evidence\n+\n+Use the specified bootcamp name/slug. Ask once only if absent. Resolve the actual AI OS root and target application's working tree; read its applicable instructions. The local default is the sibling `ai-os-projects/lsatu`, not code inside the vault. Different worktrees can hold newer task-specific state.\n+\n+## Benchmarks to read first\n+\n+Resolve these paths from the AI OS root:\n+\n+1. `references/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`: source coverage, teaching, actual interactions, persistence/access, accessibility and validation boundaries.\n+2. `references/intro-to-lr-product.md`: observed current comparison surfaces and historical intent limits.\n+3. `references/intro-to-lr-design.md`: imported scoped style evidence and the cream-versus-legacy-dark conflict.\n+4. `references/lsat-teaching-voice-standard.md`: Joshua's current selected instructional voice.\n+\n+These three benchmark replacements were reconstructed on 2026-10-11 from identified existing documents/code. They are usable current references, not recovered historical originals or proof of live acceptance. Check their owning source paths and current imports before relying on numeric values. If a source is absent, disclose that limited comparison rather than inventing a standard. If a relevant entity/product page exists, read it; absence is a documentation gap, not proof of a missing feature.\n+\n+## Inspect a focused comparison\n+\n+1. Locate the target source in the actual app checkout. Compare meaningful equivalents against `src/bootcamps/main-conclusion/`: overview/learning flow, drill/review, source/data layer and saved progress. Do not infer a feature is absent only because folders differ.\n+2. Identify the current route mount and imported styles. Historical `/bootcamp/structure`, standalone no-auth plans and dark `tokens.css` cannot override current `/bootcamp/intro-to-lr` routes or imported cream `scoped.css`.\n+3. Sample relevant components and tests, then use a signed-in browser when authorized and available. Preserve live/backend authority. Code, old audit results, deployed rendering and real account persistence are different evidence levels.\n+4. Diagnose only. If asked to fix, follow the owning app's workflow and authorization. Do not change design/product decisions merely to obtain visual sameness.\n+\n+## Report five deltas\n+\n+```text\n+1. Feature parity: useful analogous capabilities missing from the target.\n+2. Visual parity: accidental deviations from current imported styles versus deliberate differences.\n+3. Content/pedagogy parity: reasoning, feedback, source coverage and teaching-standard gaps.\n+4. Data/quality gaps: dead interactions, fabricated state, persistence/access/error handling.\n+5. Verdict: shipped-quality / in-progress / needs-work, evidence limits and the highest-value next fix.\n+```\n+\n+State checkout/commit, inspected surfaces and checks actually run. Qualify source-only review and unverified live acceptance. Keep findings short; use no em dashes. An undocumented target is a documentation gap to report, not a reason to silently skip it. Activate through the host's skill picker or a plain-language request; `$bootcamp-parity-audit` is Codex syntax, not a universal slash command.\n+'''\n+\n+CONTRACT = '''# Recovered research field contract\n+\n+The original validator was recovered verbatim from Git commit `9b1a3aa3`, path `skills/codex/research/validate_json.py`. The immutable recovery copy and SHA-256 are recorded in `audits/skills-2026-10-11-dependencies/`. No original fields.yaml dataset was found; each research task supplies its own field definitions in this recovered format.\n+\n+```yaml\n+field_categories:\n+  - category: basic_info\n+    fields:\n+      - name: name\n+        required: true\n+        description: The product or subject name\n+      - name: description\n+        required: false\n+        description: A sourced description\n+```\n+\n+The result can be flat (`name`, `description`, `uncertain`) or grouped under its schema category name (`basic_info`). The recovered historical category aliases (Basic Info, Technical Features, Performance Metrics and others) remain supported. Field names are unique and reserved metadata names `_source_file`/`uncertain` cannot be schema fields. An empty schema is invalid.\n+\n+`--require-complete` requires every defined field, rejects unknown fields and checks the root uncertain array against [uncertain] markers within field values. Use `uncertain: []` when no fields are marked. Legacy default mode still requires only required fields and reports optional coverage; research-deep always uses strict mode. An explicitly missing input path, no JSON inputs, malformed schema/JSON, duplicate keys, or an array instead of a result object fails with nonzero status.\n+\n+The validator does not certify factual accuracy, language, citation support, whether an empty value contains useful research, or current live-system truth. These require source review. Only schema categories and historical aliases are wrappers; arbitrary nested structures are field values. Do not reinterpret a different fields format without explicitly converting it and preserving the original.\n+\n+Free isolated usage:\n+\n+```text\n+uv run --no-project --with pyyaml==6.0.3 python \"<installed-skill>/scripts/validate_json.py\" --require-complete -f \"<topic>/fields.yaml\" -j \"<topic>/results/item.json\"\n+```\n+'''\n+\n+\n+def main():\n+    REPORT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n+    original = subprocess.check_output(['git', 'show', '9b1a3aa3:skills/codex/research/validate_json.py'], cwd=ROOT)\n+    recovery = REPORT / 'recovered-original-validate_json.py'\n+    if not recovery.exists():\n+        recovery.write_bytes(original)\n+    elif recovery.read_bytes() != original:\n+        raise RuntimeError('Recovered original changed')\n+    support = (ROOT / 'skills/codex/research-deep/scripts/validate_json.py').read_bytes()\n+    roots = [ROOT / 'skills/claude', ROOT / 'skills/codex', ROOT / 'skills/gemini',\n+             ROOT / '.agents/skills', ROOT / '.claude/skills', ROOT / '.gemini/skills',\n+             HOME / '.agents/skills', HOME / '.codex/skills', HOME / '.claude/skills']\n+    records = []\n+    def write(path, content):\n+        before hashlib.sha256(str(path).encode()).hexdigest() / path.name\n+            backup.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n+            if not backup.exists():\n+                backup.write_bytes(before)\n+        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n+        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(dir=path.parent, delete=False) as stream:\n+            stream.write(content)\n+            staging = Path(stream.name)\n+        current = path.read_bytes() if path.exists() else None\n+        if current != before:\n+            raise hashlib.sha256(before).hexdigest() if before else None,\n+                        'after_sha256': hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest(), 'backup': str(backup) if backup else None})\n+    for root in roots:\n+        for name, text in [('research-deep', RESEARCH), ('bootcamp-parity-audit', BOOTCAMP)]:\n+            entry = root / name / 'SKILL.md'\n+            if not entry.exists():\n+                continue\n+            write(entry, text.encode())\n+            if name == 'research-deep':\n+                write(entry.parent / 'scripts/validate_json.py', support)\n+                write(entry.parent / 'references/field-contract.md', CONTRACT.encode())\n+    evidence = []\n+    for relative in ['docs/main-conclusion-bootcamp/MAIN_CONCLUSION_HANDOFF.md',\n+ 'dependency-receipts.json').write_text(json.dumps({\n+        'date': '2026-10-11', 'original_commit': '9b1a3aa3', 'original_path': 'skills/codex/research/validate_json.py',\n+ Downloads with yt-dlp, extracts auto-scaled frames with ffmpeg, pulls the transcript from captions (or Whisper API fallback), and hands the result to Claude so it can answer questions about what\'s in the video.\\r\\nargument-hint: \\"<video-url-or-path> [question]\\"\\r\\nallowed-tools: Bash, Read, AskUserQuestion\\r\\nhomepage: https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video\\r\\nrepository: https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video\\r\\nauthor: bradautomates\\r\\nlicense: MIT\\r\\nuser-invocable: true\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n## Local execution contract (2026-10-10)\\r\\n\\r\\nUse current free get_youtube_transcript.py or native yt-dlp captions first, no Whisper key prerequisite for captions. Paid transcription fallback needs explicit provider/cost authorization and central project .env secrets. Preserve downloaded captures; cleanup only after explicit deletion authorization.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Workflow\\r\\n\\r\\n# /watch — Claude watches a video\\r\\n\\r\\nYou don\'t have a video input; this skill gives you one. A Python script downloads the video, extracts frames as JPEGs, gets a timestamped transcript (native captions first, then Whisper API as fallback), and prints frame paths. You then `Read` each frame path to see the images and combine them with the transcript to answer the user.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Step 0 — Setup preflight (runs every `/watch` invocation, silent on success)\\r\\n\\r\\n**Python interpreter:** every `python3 ...` command in this skill is for macOS/Linux. On **Windows**, substitute `python` — the `python3` command on Windows is the Microsoft Store stub and will not run the script.\\r\\n\\r\\nBefore execution check installed Python, yt-dlp and FFmpeg without reinstalling. An API key is not required for captions or frames. Use current project get_youtube_transcript.py first for transcript-only requests. For frame extraction run the bundled watch script with --no-whisper. Skip the bundled setup wizard because it couples free extraction to a provider key.\\r\\n\\r\\n## When to use\\r\\n\\r\\n- User pastes a video URL (YouTube, Vimeo, X, TikTok, Twitch clip, most yt-dlp-supported sites) and asks about it.\\r\\n- User points at a local video file (`.mp4`, `.mov`, `.mkv`, `.webm`, etc.) and asks about it.\\r\\n- User types `/watch <url-or-path> [question]`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Recommended limits\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Best accuracy: videos under 10 minutes.** Frame coverage scales inversely with duration.\\r\\n- **Hard caps: 100 frames total and 2 fps.** Token cost grows with frame count, so the script targets a frame budget by duration (and never exceeds 2 fps even when the budget would imply more):\\r\\n  - ≤30s → ~1-2 fps (up to 30 frames)\\r\\n  - 30s-1min → ~40 frames\\r\\n  - 1-3min → ~60 frames\\r\\n  - 3-10min → ~80 frames\\r\\n  - \\\\>10min → 100 frames, sparsely spaced (warning printed)\\r\\n- If the user hands you a long video, consider asking whether they want a specific section before burning tokens on a sparse scan.\\r\\n\\r\\n## How to invoke\\r\\n\\r\\n**Step 1 — parse the user input.** Separate the video source (URL or path) from any question the user asked. Example: `/watch https://youtu.be/abc what language is this in?` → source = `https://youtu.be/abc`, question = `what language is this in?`.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Step 2 — run the watch script.** Pass the source verbatim. Do not shell-escape it yourself beyond normal quoting:\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\npython \\"<actual-installed-skill-directory>/scripts/watch.py\\" --no-whisper \\"<source>\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nOptional flags:\\r\\n- `--start T` / `--end T` — focus on a section. Accepts `SS`, `MM:SS`, or `HH:MM:SS`. When either is set, fps auto-scales denser (see \\"Focusing on a section\\" below).\\r\\n- `--max-frames N` — lower the cap for tighter token budget (e.g. `--max-frames 40`)\\r\\n- `--resolution W` — change frame width in px (default 512; bump to 1024 only if the user needs to read on-screen text)\\r\\n- `--fps F` — override auto-fps (clamped to 2 fps max)\\r\\n- `--out-dir DIR` — keep working files somewhere specific (default: an auto-generated tmp dir)\\r\\n- `--whisper groq|openai` enables only that provider after upload/cost authorization. Default: free captions only.\\r\\n- `--no-whisper` — disable the Whisper fallback entirely (frames-only if no captions)\\r\\n\\r\\n### Focusing on a section (higher frame rate)\\r\\n\\r\\nWhen the user asks about a specific moment — \\"what happens at the 2 minute mark?\\", \\"zoom into 0:45 to 1:00\\", \\"the first 10 seconds\\" — pass `--start` and/or `--end`. The script switches to focused-mode budgets, which are denser than full-video budgets (still capped at 2 fps):\\r\\n\\r\\n- ≤5s → 2 fps (up to 10 frames)\\r\\n- 5-15s → 2 fps (up to 30 frames)\\r\\n- 15-30s → ~2 fps (up to 60 frames)\\r\\n- 30-60s → ~1.2 fps (up to 80 frames)\\r\\n- 60-180s → ~0.6 fps (100 frames, capped)\\r\\n\\r\\nFocused mode is the right call for:\\r\\n- Any moment/range the user names explicitly (\\"around 2:30\\", \\"the intro\\", \\"the last 30 seconds\\").\\r\\n- Any video longer than ~10 minutes where the user\'s question is about a specific part — running focused on the relevant section is far more useful than a sparse scan of the whole thing.\\r\\n- Re-runs after a full scan didn\'t have enough detail in some region.\\r\\n\\r\\nTranscript is auto-filtered to the same range. Frame timestamps are absolute (real video timeline, not offset-from-start).\\r\\n\\r\\nExamples:\\r\\n```bash\\r\\n# Last 10 seconds of a 1 minute video\\r\\npython \\"<actual-installed-skill-directory>/scripts/watch.py\\" --no-whisper video.mp4 --start 50 --end 60\\r\\n\\r\\n# Zoom into 2:15 → 2:45 at 2 fps (60 frames)\\r\\npython \\"<actual-installed-skill-directory>/scripts/watch.py\\" --no-whisper \\"$URL\\" --start 2:15 --end 2:45 --fps 2\\r\\n\\r\\n# From 1h12m to the end of the video\\r\\npython \\"<actual-installed-skill-directory>/scripts/watch.py\\" --no-whisper \\"$URL\\" --start 1:12:00\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n**Step 3: view the frames with an available host image-viewing tool.** Inspect every frame relevant to the requested claim; disclose any unsampled ranges. Use chronological timestamps to compare images and captions. Do not claim visual verification from filenames alone.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Step 4 — answer the user.** You now have two streams of evidence:\\r\\n- **Frames** — what\'s on screen at each timestamp\\r\\n- **Transcript** — what\'s said at each timestamp. The report\'s header shows the source (`captions` = yt-dlp pulled native subs; `whisper (groq)` or `whisper (openai)` = transcribed by API).\\r\\n\\r\\nIf the user asked a specific question, answer it directly citing timestamps. If they didn\'t ask anything, summarize what happens in the video — structure, key moments, notable visuals, spoken content.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Step 5: preserve evidence.** Report the working directory and keep frames/captions/downloads. Delete only after explicit deletion authorization for the verified task-owned path.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Transcription\\r\\n\\r\\nThe script gets a timestamped transcript in one of two ways:\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **Native captions (free, preferred).** yt-dlp pulls manual or auto-generated subtitles from the source platform if available.\\r\\n2. **Whisper API fallback (optional).** Missing captions do not trigger an upload. Only an explicitly selected `--whisper groq` or `--whisper openai` enables that provider after upload and cost authorization. `--no-whisper` always disables it.\\r\\n\\r\\nKeep keys in the current project central .env/environment. The repaired loader no longer reads the legacy ~/.config/watch/.env store. Do not run the legacy installer. Paid fallback needs explicit provider, upload and cost authorization; ordinary extraction remains free.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Failure modes and handling\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Setup preflight failed:** Check installed dependencies and PATH first. Do not run the legacy key-coupled installer. Provider setup is separate and only follows an explicitly authorized provider request; keys stay private in the current project central .env/environment.\\r\\n- **No transcript available** → captions missing AND (no Whisper key OR Whisper API failed). Script reports missing captions without automatically setting up a provider. Proceed frames-only and tell the user.\\r\\n- **Long video warning printed** → acknowledge it in your answer. Offer to re-run focused on a specific section via `--start`/`--end` rather than a sparse full-video scan.\\r\\n- **Download fails** → yt-dlp\'s error goes to stderr. If it\'s a login-required or region-locked video, tell the user plainly; do not keep retrying.\\r\\n- **Whisper request fails** → the error is printed to stderr (likely: invalid key, rate limit, or 25 MB upload limit on a very long video). The report will say \\"none available\\" for transcript. Do not change paid providers or retry billable transcription without that provider/cost authorization. Free captions/frames remain available.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Token efficiency\\r\\n\\r\\nThis skill burns tokens primarily on frames. Order of magnitude:\\r\\n- 80 frames at 512px wide is roughly 50-80k image tokens depending on aspect ratio.\\r\\n- The transcript is cheap (a few thousand tokens at most for a 10-minute video).\\r\\n- Bumping `--resolution` to 1024 roughly quadruples the image tokens per frame. Only do it when necessary.\\r\\n\\r\\nIf you already watched a video this session and the user asks a follow-up, do **not** re-run the script — you already have the frames and transcript in context. Just answer from what you have.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Security & Permissions\\r\\n\\r\\n**What this skill does:**\\r\\n- Runs `yt-dlp` locally to download the video and pull native captions when the source supports them (public data; the request goes directly to whatever host the URL points at)\\r\\n- Runs `ffmpeg` / `ffprobe` locally to extract frames as JPEGs and, when Whisper is needed, a mono 16 kHz audio clip\\r\\n- Sends the extracted audio clip to Groq\'s Whisper API (`api.groq.com/openai/v1/audio/transcriptions`) only when `--whisper groq` is explicitly selected and authorized\\r\\n- Sends the extracted audio clip to OpenAI\'s audio transcription API (`api.openai.com/v1/audio/transcriptions`) only when `--whisper openai` is explicitly selected and authorized\\r\\n- Writes the downloaded video, frames, audio, and an intermediate transcript to a working directory under the system temp dir (or `--out-dir` if specified) so Claude can `Read` them\\r\\n- Legacy setup reads/creates ~/.config/watch/.env; it is not run in this workflow. Extraction uses --no-whisper by default. Explicitly approved paid fallback may read central cwd .env/environment after actual helper contract verification; no new secret store is created.\\r\\n\\r\\n**What this skill does NOT do:**\\r\\n- Does not upload the video itself to any API — only the extracted audio goes out, and only when native captions are missing AND a provider was explicitly selected with `--whisper`, authorized, and not disabled with `--no-whisper`\\r\\n- Does not access any platform account (no login, no session cookies, no posting)\\r\\n- Does not share API keys between providers (Groq key only goes to `api.groq.com`, OpenAI key only goes to `api.openai.com`)\\r\\n- Does not log, cache, or write API keys to stdout, stderr, or output files\\r\\n- Keeps task output in its working directory. Preserve frames, captions and downloads; removal requires explicit authorization for the verified task-owned path.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Bundled scripts:** `scripts/watch.py` (entry point), `scripts/download.py` (yt-dlp wrapper), `scripts/frames.py` (ffmpeg frame extraction), `scripts/transcribe.py` (caption selection + Whisper orchestration), `scripts/whisper.py` (Groq / OpenAI clients), `scripts/setup.py` (preflight + installer)\\r\\n\\r\\nReview scripts before first use to verify behavior.\\r\\n\\r\\n"}}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"status":"fulfilled","value":{"chunk_id":"d82ca3","wall_time_seconds":1.942996,"exit_code":1,"original_token_count":75,"output":"Get-Content: \\r\\nLine |\\r\\n   2 |  Get-Content C:/Users/Joshu/.claude/skills/watch/hooks/watch-env-check …\\r\\n     |  ~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\\r\\n     | Cannot find path \'C:\\\\Users\\\\Joshu\\\\.claude\\\\skills\\\\watch\\\\hooks\\\\watch-env-check.sh\' because it does not exist.\\r\\n"}}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"status":"fulfilled","value":{"chunk_id":"954008","wall_time_seconds":1.2921905,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":2720,"output":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\r\\n\\"\\"\\"Setup / preflight for /watch.\\r\\n\\r\\nModes:\\r\\n  setup.py --check      Silent preflight. max(0.0, effective_end - effective_start)\\n    focused = start_sec is not None or end_sec is not None\\n\\n    if focused:\\n        fps, target = auto_fps_focus(effective_duration, max(1, int(round(fps * effective_duration)))\\n\\n    scope = (\\n        f\\"{format_time(effective_start)}-{format_time(effective_end)} ({effective_duration:.1f}s)\\"\\n        if focused else f\\"full {effective_duration:.1f}s\\"\\n    )\\n    print(f\\"[watch] extracting ~{target} frames at {fps:.3f} fps over {scope}…\\", file=sys.stderr)\\n\\n    frames = extract(\\n Preserve output; remove only with explicit authorization._\\")\\n\\n    return 0\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\"__main__\\":\\n    raise SystemExit(main())\\n\\r\\n"}}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"status":"fulfilled","value":{"chunk_id":"97551d","wall_time_seconds":1.1189383,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1485,"output":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\r\\n\\"\\"\\"Transcribe a video via Groq or OpenAI Whisper API.\\r\\n\\r\\nStrategy: extract audio (mono 16kHz mp3, tiny payload), upload to whichever\\r\\nAPI has a key. Returns segments in the same shape as transcribe.parse_vtt so\\r\\nthe rest of the pipeline (filter_range, format_transcript) doesn\'t care where\\r\\nthe transcript came from.\\r\\n\\r\\nPure stdlib — no `pip install groq` or `pip install openai` needed.\\r\\n\\"\\"\\"\\r\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\r\\n\\r\\nimport io\\r\\nimport json\\r\\nimport mimetypes\\r\\nimport os\\r\\nimport shutil\\r\\nimport ssl\\r\\nimport subprocess\\r\\nimport sys\\r\\nimport time\\r\\nimport urllib.error\\r\\nimport uuid\\r\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\r\\nfrom urllib.request import Request, urlopen\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nGROQ_ENDPOINT = \\"https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/audio/transcriptions\\"\\r\\nGROQ_MODEL = \\"whisper-large-v3\\"\\r\\n\\r\\nOPENAI_ENDPOINT = \\"https://api.openai.com/v1/audio/transcriptions\\"\\r\\nOPENAI_MODEL = \\"whisper-1\\"\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef load_api_key(preferred: str | None = None) -> tuple[str, str] | tuple[None, None]:\\r\\n    \\"\\"\\"Return (backend, api_key). Prefers Groq, falls back to OpenAI.\\r\\n\\r\\n    If `preferred` is \\"groq\\" or \\"openai\\", only that backend\'s key is considered.\\r\\n    \\"\\"\\"\\r\\n    def _from_env(name: str) -> str | None:\\r\\n        value = os.environ.get(name)\\r\\n        return value.strip() if value else None\\r\\n\\r\\n    def _from_dotenv(path: Path, name: str) -> str | None:\\r\\n        if not path.exists():\\r\\n            return None\\r\\n        try:\\r\\n            for line in path.read_text(encoding=\\"utf-8\\").splitlines():\\r\\n                line = line.strip()\\r\\n                if not line or line.startswith(\\"#\\") or \\"=\\" not in line:\\r\\n                    continue\\r\\n                key, _, value = line.partition(\\"=\\")\\r\\n                if key.strip() != name:\\r\\n                    continue\\r\\n                value = value.strip()\\r\\n                if len(value) >= 2 and value[0] in (\'\\"\', \\"\'\\") and value[-1] == value[0]:\\r\\n                    value = value[1:-1]\\r\\n                return value or None\\r\\n        except OSError:\\r\\n            return None\\r\\n        return None\\r\\n\\r\\n    dotenv_paths = [\\r\\n        Path.cwd() / \\".env\\",\\r\\n    ]\\r\\n\\r\\n    candidates = ((\\"GROQ_API_KEY\\", \\"groq\\"), (\\"OPENAI_API_KEY\\", \\"openai\\"))\\r\\n    if preferred is not None:\\r\\n        candidates = tuple(c for c in candidates if c[1] == preferred)\\r\\n\\r\\n    for key_name, backend in candidates:\\r\\n        value = _from_env(key_name)\\r\\n        if not value:\\r\\n            for candidate in dotenv_paths:\\r\\n                value = _from_dotenv(candidate, key_name)\\r\\n                if value:\\r\\n                    break\\r\\n        if value:\\r\\n            return backend, value\\r\\n\\r\\n    return None, None\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef extract_audio(video_path: str, out_path: Path) -> Path:\\r\\n    \\"\\"\\"Extract mono 16kHz 64kbps mp3 — ~480 kB/min, fits any Whisper limit.\\"\\"\\"\\r\\n    if shutil.which(\\"ffmpeg\\") is None:\\r\\n        raise SystemExit(\\"ffmpeg is not installed. Check installed FFmpeg and refresh PATH before installing for your platform\\")\\r\\n\\r\\n    if out_path.exists():\\r\\n        raise SystemExit(\\"Audio output already exists; choose a fresh path\\")\\r\\n    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\r\\n    cmd = [\\r\\n        \\"ffmpeg\\",\\r\\n        \\"-hide_banner\\",\\r\\n        \\"-loglevel\\", \\"error\\",\\r\\n        \\"-n\\",\\r\\n        \\"-i\\", str(Path(video_path).resolve()),\\r\\n        \\"-vn\\",\\r\\n        \\"-acodec\\", \\"libmp3lame\\",\\r\\n        \\"-ar\\", \\"16000\\",\\r\\n        \\"-ac\\", \\"1\\",\\r\\n        \\"-b:a\\", \\"64k\\",\\r\\n        str(out_path.resolve()),\\r\\n    ]\\r\\n    result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)\\r\\n    if result.returncode != 0:\\r\\n        raise SystemExit(f\\"ffmpeg audio extraction failed: {result.stderr.strip()}\\")\\r\\n    if not out_path.exists() or out_path.stat().st_size == 0:\\r\\n        raise SystemExit(\\"ffmpeg produced no audio — video may have no audio track\\")\\r\\n    return out_path\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef _build_multipart(fields: dict[str, str], file_path: Path) -> tuple[bytes, str]:\\r\\n    \\"\\"\\"Assemble a multipart/form-data body the Whisper APIs accept.\\r\\n\\r\\n    Whisper\'s multipart upload is small and predictable — doing it by hand\\r\\n    keeps us on pure stdlib instead of pulling requests/groq/openai SDKs.\\r\\n    \\"\\"\\"\\r\\n    boundary = f\\"----WatchBoundary{uuid.uuid4().hex}\\"\\r\\n    eol = b\\"\\\\r\\\\n\\"\\r\\n    buf = io.BytesIO()\\r\\n\\r\\n    for name, value in fields.items():\\r\\n        buf.write(f\\"--{boundary}\\".encode()); buf.write(eol)\\r\\n        buf.write(f\'Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\\"{name}\\"\'.encode()); buf.write(eol)\\r\\n        buf.write(eol)\\r\\n        buf.write(str(value).encode()); buf.write(eol)\\r\\n\\r\\n    mimetype = mimetypes.guess_type(file_path.name)[0] or \\"application/octet-stream\\"\\r\\n    buf.write(f\\"--{boundary}\\".encode()); buf.write(eol)\\r\\n    buf.write(\\r\\n        f\'Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\\"file\\"; filename=\\"{file_path.name}\\"\'.encode()\\r\\n    )\\r\\n    buf.write(eol)\\r\\n    buf.write(f\\"Content-Type: {mimetype}\\".encode()); buf.write(eol)\\r\\n    buf.write(eol)\\r\\n    buf.write(file_path.read_bytes())\\r\\n    buf.write(eol)\\r\\n    buf.write(f\\"--{boundary}--\\".encode()); buf.write(eol)\\r\\n\\r\\n    return buf.getvalue(), boundary\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nMAX_ATTEMPTS = 4       # initial + 3 retries\\r\\nMAX_429_RETRIES = 2\\r\\nRETRY_BASE_DELAY = 2.0\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef _post_whisper(endpoint: str, api_key: [REDACTED], model: str, audio_path: Path) -> dict:\\r\\n    fields = {\\r\\n        \\"model\\": model,\\r\\n        \\"response_format\\": \\"verbose_json\\",\\r\\n        \\"temperature\\": \\"0\\",\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    body, boundary = _build_multipart(fields, audio_path)\\r\\n    headers = {\\r\\n        \\"Authorization\\": f\\"Bearer {api_key}\\",\\r\\n        \\"Content-Type\\": f\\"multipart/form-data; boundary={boundary}\\",\\r\\n        # Groq sits behind Cloudflare — the default `Python-urllib/3.x` UA\\r\\n        # trips WAF rule 1010 (403) before auth even runs. Any non-default\\r\\n        # UA clears it; we identify honestly.\\r\\n        \\"User-Agent\\": \\"watch-skill/1.0 (+claude-code; python-urllib)\\",\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    context = ssl.create_default_context()\\r\\n    rate_limit_hits = 0\\r\\n    last_exc: Exception | None = None\\r\\n    last_detail = \\"\\"\\r\\n\\r\\n    for attempt in range(MAX_ATTEMPTS):\\r\\n"}}'}]

proc.stdin.write(json.dumps(value) + \'\\\\n\')\\n        proc.stdin.flush()\\n    def response(ident):\\n        deadline = time.monotonic() + 45\\n        while time.monotonic() < deadline:\\n watch preflight payloads only.\\"\\"\\"\\nimport hashlib\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / \'ai-os\'\\nREPORT = ROOT / \'audits/skills-2026-10-11-native/watch-preflight\'\\nSETUP = \'\'\'#!/usr/bin/env python3\\n\\"\\"\\"Read-only watch preflight. Native captions need no paid provider key.\\"\\"\\"\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport shutil\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nREQUIRED_BINARIES = (\\"ffmpeg\\", \\"ffprobe\\", \\"yt-dlp\\")\\n\\ndef central_root():\\n    \\"\\"\\"Resolve the workspace root from its paired operating instructions.\\"\\"\\"\\n    for candidate in (Path.cwd(), *Path.cwd().parents):\\n        if (candidate / \\"AGENTS.md\\").is_file() and (candidate / \\"CLAUDE.md\\").is_file():\\n            return candidate\\n    return None\\n\\ndef credential_present(provider):\\n    name = {\\"groq\\": \\"GROQ_API_KEY\\", \\"openai\\": \\"OPENAI_API_KEY\\"}[provider]\\n    if os.environ.get(name, \\"\\").strip():\\n        return True\\n    root = central_root()\\n    if root is None:\\n        return False\\n    try:\\n        lines = (root / \\".env\\").read_text(encoding=\\"utf-8\\").splitlines()\\n    except OSError:\\n        return False\\n    for line in lines:\\n        key, separator, value = line.strip().partition(\\"=\\")\\n        if separator and key.strip() == name:\\n            return bool(value.strip().strip(\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\'\\"))\\n    return False\\n\\ndef _status(paid_provider=None):\\n    missing = [name for name in REQUIRED_BINARIES if shutil.which(name) is None]\\n    result = {\\"status\\": \\"needs_tools\\" if missing else \\"ready\\", \\"mode\\": \\"native_captions\\",\\n              \\"missing_binaries\\": missing, \\"paid_provider\\": paid_provider}\\n    if paid_provider:\\n        result[\\"credential_present\\"] = credential_present(paid_provider)\\n        result[\\"mode\\"] = \\"explicit_paid_provider_preflight\\"\\n        if not missing and not result[\\"credential_present\\"]:\\n            result[\\"status\\"] = \\"needs_selected_provider_key\\"\\n    return result\\n\\ndef main(argv=None):\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\\n    output = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group()\\n    output.add_argument(\\"--check\\", action=\\"store_true\\")\\n    output.add_argument(\\"--json\\", action=\\"store_true\\")\\n    output.add_argument(\\"--hook\\", action=\\"store_true\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\"--paid-provider\\", choices=(\\"groq\\", \\"openai\\"),\\n                        help=\\"Check selected credential presence only; does not authorize or call an API\\")\\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\\n    status = _status(args.paid_provider)\\n    if args.json:\\n        print(json.dumps(status, sort_keys=True))\\n    elif status[\\"missing_binaries\\"]:\\n        print(\\"/watch: missing tools on PATH: \\" + \\", \\".join(status[\\"missing_binaries\\"]) +\\n              \\". Check existing installs and refresh PATH; this preflight installs nothing.\\")\\n    elif status[\\"status\\"] == \\"needs_selected_provider_key\\":\\n        print(\\"/watch: selected provider credential absent. Use the AI OS root .env or process environment only. Native captions remain free; no provider was called.\\")\\n    elif not args.hook:\\n        print(\\"/watch: ready for native captions and local frames. No API key required; no files changed.\\")\\n    return 2 if status[\\"missing_binaries\\"] else (3 if status[\\"status\\"] == \\"needs_selected_provider_key\\" else 0)\\n\\nif __name__ == \\"__main__\\":\\n    raise SystemExit(main())\\n\'\'\'\\nHOOK = \'\'\'#!/usr/bin/env bash\\n# Read-only SessionStart preflight. No credentials, installs, or scaffolding.\\nset -euo pipefail\\nSCRIPT_DIR=\\"$(cd -- \\"$(dirname -- \\"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\\")\\" && pwd)\\"\\nif command -v python >/dev/null 2>&1; then\\n  python \\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../../scripts/setup.py\\" --hook\\nelif command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1; then\\n  python3 \\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../../scripts/setup.py\\" --hook\\nelse\\n  echo \\"/watch: Python is not on PATH. Check the existing install and refresh PATH; nothing was installed.\\"\\nfi\\n\'\'\'\\n\\ndef main():\\n    roots = [ROOT/\'skills\'/runtime/\'watch\' for runtime in (\'claude\',\'codex\',\'gemini\')]\\n    roots += [ROOT/f\'.{runtime}\'/\'skills/watch\' for runtime in (\'agents\',\'claude\',\'gemini\')]\\n    roots += [Path.home()/runtime/\'skills/watch\' for runtime in (\'.agents\',\'.codex\')]\\n    REPORT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n    receipts = []\\n    for root in roots:\\n        if not (root/\'scripts/setup.py\').is_file():\\n            continue\\n        changes = {root/\'scripts/setup.py\': SETUP}\\n        definition = root/\'hooks/hooks.json\'\\n        if definition.is_file():\\n            before_definition = definition.read_text(encoding=\'utf-8\')\\n            changes[definition] = before_definition.replace(\'bash ${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/hooks/scripts/check-setup.sh\', \'bash \\"${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/hooks/scripts/check-setup.sh\\"\')\\n            # JSON encoding must escape the quotes inside command strings.\\n            parsed = json.loads(before_definition)\\n            for stage in parsed.get(\'hooks\', {}).values():\\n                for group in stage:\\n                    for hook in group.get(\'hooks\', []):\\n                        if \'check-setup.sh\' in hook.get(\'command\', \'\'):\\n                            hook[\'command\'] = \'bash \\"${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/hooks/scripts/check-setup.sh\\"\'\\n            changes[definition] = json.dumps(parsed, indent=2) + \'\\\\n\'\\n            changes[root/\'hooks/scripts/check-setup.sh\'] = HOOK\\n        for path, body in changes.items():\\n            previous = path.read_bytes() if path.exists() else None\\n            encoded = body.encode(\'utf-8\')\\n            if previous == encoded:\\n                continue\\n            identity = hashlib.sha256(str(path).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]\\n            if previous is not None:\\n                (REPORT/f\'{identity}.before\').write_bytes(previous)\\n            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n            temp = path.with_name(path.name+\'.preflight-tmp\')\\n            temp.write_bytes(encoded)\\n            assert (path.read_bytes() if path.exists() else None) == previous, str(path)\\n            os.replace(temp, path)\\n            assert path.read_bytes() == encoded\\n            receipts.append({\'path\':str(path),\'before\': hashlib.sha256(previous).hexdigest() if previous is not None else None,\'after\':hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest(),\'backup\':str(REPORT/f\'{identity}.before\') if previous is not None else None})\\n    (REPORT/\'receipts.json\').write_text(json.dumps(receipts,indent=2)+\'\\\\n\',encoding=\'utf-8\')\\n    print(json.dumps({\'changed\':len(receipts),\'report\':str(REPORT)}))\\n\\nif __name__ == \'__main__\':\\n    main()\\n\\r\\n"}}'}]

'{"status":"fulfilled","value":{"chunk_id":"91be60","wall_time_seconds":1.4508096,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1656,"output":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\r\\n\\"\\"\\"Probe video metadata and extract frames at an auto-scaled fps.\\r\\n\\r\\nAuto-fps targets a frame budget, not a fixed rate. Token cost scales with frame\\r\\ncount, so budget-by-duration keeps short videos dense and long videos capped.\\r\\nWhen a user-specified range is passed, focused-mode budgets denser (they are\\r\\nzooming in for detail).\\r\\n\\"\\"\\"\\r\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\r\\n\\r\\nimport json\\r\\nimport shutil\\r\\nimport subprocess\\r\\nimport sys\\r\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nMAX_FPS = 2.0\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef _clamp_fps(fps: float, duration_seconds: float, max_frames: int) -> tuple[float, int]:\\r\\n    fps = min(fps, MAX_FPS)\\r\\n    target = min(max_frames, max(1, int(round(fps * duration_seconds))))\\r\\n    return fps, target\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef parse_time(value: str | float | int | None) -> float | None:\\r\\n    \\"\\"\\"Parse SS, MM:SS, or HH:MM:SS (with optional .ms) into seconds.\\"\\"\\"\\r\\n    if value is None:\\r\\n        return None\\r\\n    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):\\r\\n        return float(value)\\r\\n    s = str(value).strip()\\r\\n    if not s:\\r\\n        return None\\r\\n    parts = s.split(\\":\\")\\r\\n    try:\\r\\n        if len(parts) == 1:\\r\\n            return float(parts[0])\\r\\n        if len(parts) == 2:\\r\\n            return int(parts[0]) * 60 + float(parts[1])\\r\\n        if len(parts) == 3:\\r\\n            return int(parts[0]) * 3600 + int(parts[1]) * 60 + float(parts[2])\\r\\n    except ValueError:\\r\\n        pass\\r\\n    raise SystemExit(f\\"Cannot parse time value: {value!r} (expected SS, MM:SS, or HH:MM:SS)\\")\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef format_time(seconds: float) -> str:\\r\\n    total = int(round(seconds))\\r\\n    hours, rem = divmod(total, 3600)\\r\\n    minutes, sec = divmod(rem, 60)\\r\\n    if hours:\\r\\n        return f\\"{hours}:{minutes:02d}:{sec:02d}\\"\\r\\n    return f\\"{minutes:02d}:{sec:02d}\\"\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef get_metadata(video_path: str) -> dict:\\r\\n    if shutil.which(\\"ffprobe\\") is None:\\r\\n        raise SystemExit(\\"ffprobe is not installed. Check the installed FFmpeg location and refresh PATH before installing for your platform\\")\\r\\n\\r\\n    result = subprocess.run(\\r\\n        [\\r\\n            \\"ffprobe\\",\\r\\n            \\"-v\\", \\"quiet\\",\\r\\n            \\"-print_format\\", \\"json\\",\\r\\n            \\"-show_format\\",\\r\\n            \\"-show_streams\\",\\r\\n            str(Path(video_path).resolve()),\\r\\n        ],\\r\\n        capture_output=True,\\r\\n        text=True,\\r\\n    )\\r\\n    if result.returncode != 0:\\r\\n        raise SystemExit(f\\"ffprobe failed: {result.stderr.strip()}\\")\\r\\n\\r\\n    data = json.loads(result.stdout or \\"{}\\")\\r\\n    streams = data.get(\\"streams\\", [])\\r\\n    fmt = data.get(\\"format\\", {})\\r\\n    video_stream = next((s for s in streams if s.get(\\"codec_type\\") == \\"video\\"), {})\\r\\n    audio_stream = next((s for s in streams if s.get(\\"codec_type\\") == \\"audio\\"), None)\\r\\n\\r\\n    duration = float(fmt.get(\\"duration\\") or video_stream.get(\\"duration\\") or 0)\\r\\n    return {\\r\\n        \\"duration_seconds\\": duration,\\r\\n        \\"width\\": video_stream.get(\\"width\\"),\\r\\n        \\"height\\": video_stream.get(\\"height\\"),\\r\\n        \\"codec\\": video_stream.get(\\"codec_name\\"),\\r\\n        \\"size_bytes\\": int(fmt.get(\\"size\\") or 0),\\r\\n        \\"has_audio\\": audio_stream is not None,\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef auto_fps(duration_seconds: float, max_frames: int = 100) -> tuple[float, int]:\\r\\n    \\"\\"\\"Pick fps that targets a sensible frame budget for full-video scans.\\"\\"\\"\\r\\n    if duration_seconds <= 0:\\r\\n        return 1.0, 1\\r\\n\\r\\n    if duration_seconds <= 30:\\r\\n        target = min(max_frames, max(12, int(round(duration_seconds))))\\r\\n    elif duration_seconds <= 60:\\r\\n        target = min(max_frames, 40)\\r\\n    elif duration_seconds <= 180:  # 3 min\\r\\n        target = min(max_frames, 60)\\r\\n    elif duration_seconds <= 600:  # 10 min\\r\\n        target = min(max_frames, 80)\\r\\n    else:\\r\\n        target = max_frames\\r\\n\\r\\n    return _clamp_fps(target / duration_seconds, duration_seconds, max_frames)\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef auto_fps_focus(duration_seconds: float, max_frames: int = 100) -> tuple[float, int]:\\r\\n    \\"\\"\\"Denser budget for user-specified ranges — they are zooming in for detail.\\"\\"\\"\\r\\n    if duration_seconds <= 0:\\r\\n        return min(MAX_FPS, 2.0), 2\\r\\n\\r\\n    if duration_seconds <= 5:\\r\\n        target = min(max_frames, max(10, int(round(duration_seconds * 6))))\\r\\n    elif duration_seconds <= 15:\\r\\n        target = min(max_frames, max(30, int(round(duration_seconds * 4))))\\r\\n    elif duration_seconds <= 30:\\r\\n        target = min(max_frames, 60)\\r\\n    elif duration_seconds <= 60:\\r\\n        target = min(max_frames, 80)\\r\\n    elif duration_seconds <= 180:\\r\\n        target = max_frames\\r\\n    else:\\r\\n        target = max_frames\\r\\n\\r\\n    return _clamp_fps(target / duration_seconds, duration_seconds, max_frames)\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef extract(\\r\\n    video_path: str,\\r\\n    out_dir: Path,\\r\\n    fps: float,\\r\\n    resolution: int = 512,\\r\\n    max_frames: int = 100,\\r\\n    start_seconds: float | None = None,\\r\\n    end_seconds: float | None = None,\\r\\n) -> list[dict]:\\r\\n    if shutil.which(\\"ffmpeg\\") is None:\\r\\n        raise SystemExit(\\"ffmpeg is not installed. Check the installed FFmpeg location and refresh PATH before installing for your platform\\")\\r\\n\\r\\n    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\r\\n    if any(out_dir.iterdir()):\\r\\n        raise SystemExit(\\"Frame output directory is not empty; existing files were preserved\\")\\r\\n\\r\\n    output_pattern = str(out_dir / \\"frame_%04d.jpg\\")\\r\\n    cmd: list[str] = [\\r\\n        \\"ffmpeg\\",\\r\\n        \\"-hide_banner\\",\\r\\n        \\"-loglevel\\", \\"error\\",\\r\\n        \\"-n\\",\\r\\n    ]\\r\\n\\r\\n    # -ss before -i = fast seek (keyframe-snap, good enough for preview frames).\\r\\n    if start_seconds is not None:\\r\\n        cmd += [\\"-ss\\", f\\"{start_seconds:.3f}\\"]\\r\\n    if end_seconds is not None:\\r\\n        cmd += [\\"-to\\", f\\"{end_seconds:.3f}\\"]\\r\\n\\r\\n    cmd += [\\r\\n        \\"-i\\", str(Path(video_path).resolve()),\\r\\n        \\"-vf\\", f\\"fps={fps},scale={resolution}:-2\\",\\r\\n        \\"-frames:v\\", str(max_frames),\\r\\n        \\"-q:v\\", \\"4\\",\\r\\n        output_pattern,\\r\\n    ]\\r\\n\\r\\n    result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)\\r\\n    if result.returncode != 0:\\r\\n        raise SystemExit(f\\"ffmpeg frame extraction failed: {result.stderr.strip()}\\")\\r\\n\\r\\n    offset = start_seconds or 0.0\\r\\n    frames = sorted(out_dir.glob(\\"frame_*.jpg\\"))\\r\\n    return [\\r\\n        {\\r\\n            \\"index\\": i,\\r\\n            \\"timestamp_seconds\\": round(offset + (i / fps if fps > 0 else 0.0), 2),\\r\\n            \\"path\\": str(p),\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        for i, p in enumerate(frames)\\r\\n    ]\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nif __name__ == \\"__main__\\":\\r\\n    if len(sys.argv) < 3:\\r\\n        print(\\r\\n            \\"usage: frames.py <video-path> <out-dir> [--fps F] [--resolution W] \\"\\r\\n            \\"[--max-frames N] [--start T] [--end T]\\",\\r\\n            file=sys.stderr,\\r\\n        )\\r\\n        raise SystemExit(2)\\r\\n\\r\\n    video = sys.argv[1]\\r\\n    out = Path(sys.argv[2])\\r\\n    args = sys.argv[3:]\\r\\n\\r\\n"}}'}]

Path\\r\\n\\r\\nREQUIRED_BINARIES = (\\"ffmpeg\\", \\"ffprobe\\", \\"yt-dlp\\")\\r\\n\\r\\ndef central_root():\\r\\n    \\"\\"\\"Resolve the workspace root from its paired operating  return candidate\\r\\n    return None\\r\\n\\r\\ndef credential_present(provider):\\r\\n    name = {\\"groq\\": \\"GROQ_API_KEY\\", \\"openai\\": \\"OPENAI_API_KEY\\"}[provider]\\r\\n    if os.environ.get(name, \\"\\").strip():\\r\\n        return True\\r\\n    root = central_root()\\r\\n    if root is None:\\r\\n        return False\\r\\n    try:\\r\\n        lines = (root / \\".env\\").read_text(encoding=\\"utf-8\\").splitlines()\\r\\n    except OSError:\\r\\n        return False\\r\\n    for line in lines:\\r\\n        key, separator, value = line.strip().partition(\\"=\\")\\r\\n        if separator and key.strip() == name:\\r\\n            return bool(value.strip().strip(\\"\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\'\\"))\\r\\n    return False\\r\\n\\r\\ndef _status(paid_provider=None):\\r\\n    missing = [name for name in REQUIRED_BINARIES if shutil.which(name) is None]\\r\\n    result = {\\"status\\": \\"needs_tools\\" if missing else \\"ready\\", \\"mode\\": \\"native_captions\\",\\r\\n              \\"missing_binaries\\": missing, \\"paid_provider\\": paid_provider}\\r\\n    if paid_provider:\\r\\n        result[\\"credential_present\\"] = credential_present(paid_provider)\\r\\n        result[\\"mode\\"] = \\"explicit_paid_provider_preflight\\"\\r\\n        if not missing and not result[\\"credential_present\\"]:\\r\\n            result[\\"status\\"] = \\"needs_selected_provider_key\\"\\r\\n    return result\\r\\n\\r\\ndef main(argv=None):\\r\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\\r\\n    output = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group()\\r\\n    output.add_argument(\\"--check\\", action=\\"store_true\\")\\r\\n    output.add_argument(\\"--json\\", action=\\"store_true\\")\\r\\n    output.add_argument(\\"--hook\\", action=\\"store_true\\")\\r\\n    parser.add_argument(\\"--paid-provider\\", choices=(\\"groq\\", \\"openai\\"),\\r\\n                        help=\\"Check selected credential presence only; does not authorize or call an API\\")\\r\\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\\r\\n    status = _status(args.paid_provider)\\r\\n    if args.json:\\r\\n        print(json.dumps(status, sort_keys=True))\\r\\n    elif status[\\"missing_binaries\\"]:\\r\\n        print(\\"/watch: missing tools on PATH: \\" + \\", \\".join(status[\\"missing_binaries\\"]) +\\r\\n              \\". Use the AI OS root .env or process environment only. Native captions remain free; no provider was called.\\")\\r\\n    elif not args.hook:\\r\\n        print(\\"/watch: ready for native captions and local frames. No API key required; no files changed.\\")\\r\\n    return 2 if status[\\"missing_binaries\\"] else (3 if status[\\"status\\"] == \\"needs_selected_provider_key\\" else 0)\\r\\n\\r\\nif __name__ == \\"__main__\\":\\r\\n    raise SystemExit(main())\\r\\n\'\'\'\\r\\nHOOK = \'\'\'#!/usr/bin/env bash\\r\\n# Read-only SessionStart preflight. No credentials, installs, or scaffolding.\\r\\nset -euo pipefail\\r\\nSCRIPT_DIR=\\"$(cd -- \\"$(dirname -- \\"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\\")\\" && pwd)\\"\\r\\nif command -v python >/dev/null 2>&1; then\\r\\n  python \\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../../scripts/setup.py\\" --hook\\r\\nelif command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1; then\\r\\n  python3 \\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../../scripts/setup.py\\" --hook\\r\\nelse\\r\\n  echo \\"/watch: Python is not on PATH. Check the existing install and refresh PATH; nothing was installed.\\"\\r\\nfi\\r\\n\'\'\'\\r\\n\\r\\ndef main():\\r\\n    roots = [ROOT/\'skills\'/runtime/\'watch\' for runtime in (\'claude\',\'codex\',\'gemini\')]\\r\\n    roots += [ROOT/f\'.{runtime}\'/\'skills/watch\' for runtime in (\'agents\',\'claude\',\'gemini\')]\\r\\n    roots += [Path.home()/runtime/\'skills/watch\' for runtime in (\'.agents\',\'.codex\',\'.claude\')]\\r\\n    REPORT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\r\\n    receipts = []\\r\\n    for root in roots:\\r\\n        if not (root/\'scripts/setup.py\').is_file():\\r\\n            continue\\r\\n        changes = {root/\'scripts/setup.py\': SETUP}\\r\\n        definition = root/\'hooks/hooks.json\'\\r\\n        if definition.is_file():\\r\\n            before_definition = definition.read_text(encoding=\'utf-8\')\\r\\n            changes[definition] = before_definition.replace(\'bash ${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/hooks/scripts/check-setup.sh\', \'bash \\"${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/hooks/scripts/check-setup.sh\\"\')\\r\\n            # JSON encoding must escape the quotes inside command strings.\\r\\n            parsed = json.loads(before_definition)\\r\\n            for stage in parsed.get(\'hooks\', {}).values():\\r\\n                for group in stage:\\r\\n                    for hook in group.get(\'hooks\', []):\\r\\n                        if \'check-setup.sh\' in hook.get(\'command\', \'\'):\\r\\n                            hook[\'command\'] = \'bash \\"${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/hooks/scripts/check-setup.sh\\"\'\\r\\n            changes[definition] = json.dumps(parsed, indent=2) + \'\\\\n\'\\r\\n            changes[root/\'hooks/scripts/check-setup.sh\'] = HOOK\\r\\n        for path, body in changes.items():\\r\\n            previous = path.read_bytes() if path.exists() else None\\r\\n            encoded = body.encode(\'utf-8\')\\r\\n            if previous == encoded:\\r\\n                continue\\r\\n            identity = hashlib.sha256(str(path).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]\\r\\n            if previous is not None:\\r\\n                (REPORT/f\'{identity}.before\').write_bytes(previous)\\r\\n            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\r\\n            temp = path.with_name(path.name+\'.preflight-tmp\')\\r\\n            temp.write_bytes(encoded)\\r\\n            assert (path.read_bytes() if path.exists() else None) == previous, str(path)\\r\\n            os.replace(temp, path)\\r\\n            assert path.read_bytes() == encoded\\r\\n            receipts.append({\'path\':str(path),\'before\': hashlib.sha256(previous).hexdigest() if previous is not None else None,\'after\':hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest(),\'backup\':str(REPORT/f\'{identity}.before\') if previous is not None else None})\\r\\n    (REPORT/\'receipts.json\').write_text(json.dumps(receipts,indent=2)+\'\\\\n\',encoding=\'utf-8\')\\r\\n    print(json.dumps({\'changed\':len(receipts),\'report\':str(REPORT)}))\\r\\n\\r\\nif __name__ == \'__main__\':\\r\\n    main()\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 25.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"23e762","wall_time_seconds":0.826146,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":955,"output":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\r\\n\\"\\"\\"Transcribe a video via Groq or OpenAI Whisper API.\\r\\n\\r\\nStrategy: extract audio (mono 16kHz mp3, tiny payload), upload to whichever\\r\\nAPI has a key. Returns segments in the same shape as transcribe.parse_vtt so\\r\\nthe rest of the pipeline (filter_range, format_transcript) doesn\'t care where\\r\\nthe transcript came from.\\r\\n\\r\\nPure stdlib — no `pip install groq` or `pip install openai` needed.\\r\\n\\"\\"\\"\\r\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\r\\n\\r\\nimport io\\r\\nimport json\\r\\nimport mimetypes\\r\\nimport os\\r\\nimport shutil\\r\\nimport ssl\\r\\nimport subprocess\\r\\nimport sys\\r\\nimport time\\r\\nimport urllib.error\\r\\nimport uuid\\r\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\r\\nfrom urllib.request import Request, urlopen\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nGROQ_ENDPOINT = \\"https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/audio/transcriptions\\"\\r\\nGROQ_MODEL = \\"whisper-large-v3\\"\\r\\n\\r\\nOPENAI_ENDPOINT = \\"https://api.openai.com/v1/audio/transcriptions\\"\\r\\nOPENAI_MODEL = \\"whisper-1\\"\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef load_api_key(preferred: str | None = None) -> tuple[str, str] | tuple[None, None]:\\r\\n    \\"\\"\\"Return (backend, api_key). Prefers Groq, falls back to OpenAI.\\r\\n\\r\\n    If `preferred` is \\"groq\\" or \\"openai\\", only that backend\'s key is considered.\\r\\n    \\"\\"\\"\\r\\n    def _from_env(name: str) -> str | None:\\r\\n        value = os.environ.get(name)\\r\\n        return value.strip() if value else None\\r\\n\\r\\n    def _from_dotenv(path: Path, name: str) -> str | None:\\r\\n        if not path.exists():\\r\\n            return None\\r\\n        try:\\r\\n            for line in path.read_text(encoding=\\"utf-8\\").splitlines():\\r\\n                line = line.strip()\\r\\n                if not line or line.startswith(\\"#\\") or \\"=\\" not in line:\\r\\n                    continue\\r\\n                key, _, value = line.partition(\\"=\\")\\r\\n                if key.strip() != name:\\r\\n                    continue\\r\\n                value = value.strip()\\r\\n                if len(value) >= 2 and value[0] in (\'\\"\', \\"\'\\") and value[-1] == value[0]:\\r\\n                    value = value[1:-1]\\r\\n                return value or None\\r\\n        except OSError:\\r\\n            return None\\r\\n        return None\\r\\n\\r\\n    # Use the same central workspace root as the read-only setup preflight.\\r\\n    # Never silently select a nested project credential file.\\r\\n    dotenv_paths = []\\r\\n    for candidate in (Path.cwd(), *Path.cwd().parents):\\r\\n        if (candidate / \\"AGENTS.md\\").is_file() and (candidate / \\"CLAUDE.md\\").is_file():\\r\\n            dotenv_paths = [candidate / \\".env\\"]\\r\\n            break\\r\\n\\r\\n    candidates = ((\\"GROQ_API_KEY\\", \\"groq\\"), (\\"OPENAI_API_KEY\\", \\"openai\\"))\\r\\n    if preferred is not None:\\r\\n        candidates = tuple(c for c in candidates if c[1] == preferred)\\r\\n\\r\\n    for key_name, backend in candidates:\\r\\n        value = _from_env(key_name)\\r\\n        if not value:\\r\\n            for candidate in dotenv_paths:\\r\\n                value = _from_dotenv(candidate, key_name)\\r\\n                if value:\\r\\n                    break\\r\\n        if value:\\r\\n            return backend, value\\r\\n\\r\\n    return None, None\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef extract_audio(video_path: str, out_path: Path) -> Path:\\r\\n    \\"\\"\\"Extract mono 16kHz 64kbps mp3 — ~480 kB/min, fits any Whisper limit.\\"\\"\\"\\r\\n    if shutil.which(\\"ffmpeg\\") is None:\\r\\n        raise SystemExit(\\"ffmpeg is not installed. Check installed FFmpeg and refresh PATH before installing for your platform\\")\\r\\n\\r\\n    if out_path.exists():\\r\\n        raise SystemExit(\\"Audio output already exists; choose a fresh path\\")\\r\\n    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\r\\n    cmd = [\\r\\n        \\"ffmpeg\\",\\r\\n        \\"-hide_banner\\",\\r\\n        \\"-loglevel\\", \\"error\\",\\r\\n        \\"-n\\",\\r\\n        \\"-i\\", str(Path(video_path).resolve()),\\r\\n        \\"-vn\\",\\r\\n        \\"-acodec\\", \\"libmp3lame\\",\\r\\n        \\"-ar\\", \\"16000\\",\\r\\n        \\"-ac\\", \\"1\\",\\r\\n        \\"-b:a\\", \\"64k\\",\\r\\n        str(out_path.resolve()),\\r\\n    ]\\r\\n    result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)\\r\\n    if result.returncode !=
